Private restrictions such as HOAs should take precedence over the ordinance. To To level the playing field, what I'm suggesting is that we continue to use the ICC chart for cost of construction. And that's determined on the type of construction and the square footage. [0:37:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1xq5vj2w0mI&t=2242s) The federal legislature recently this summer approved a new bill that allowed you to relocate The earmarked funds within 50 miles of the original earmark that is something That had never been allowed before without actual federal Congress taking action some cog approached me and asked me if city of South Lyon would be willing to donate our $90 ,000 to one of the other Oakland County projects that They were working on to which I said no we have plenty of street work that we We need to do, if we can relocate $90 ,000 for a street that needs work done within the City, we'll do that. We applied, had HRC put together cost estimates for a couple of federal aid areas that would benefit from this and found that the McMunn Street project between Dorothy and McHattie Was in most need and most within our budget for the 2017 year. So what we're asking now, and Mike is here to answer any questions, is permission for the funds to begin the preliminary design so that when the new fiscal year rolls around, we are ready to begin the project.

[0:38:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1xq5vj2w0mI&t=2339s) Yes, the total project cost is estimated to be $461 ,630. Of that, $90 ,000 is coming from the federal earmark.

I spoke to several different communities and townships that have actually made that transition Since 2008 and later, and if the council would like, I can provide you copies of those emails, but the general consensus was that moving to a four -day workweek for City Hall and administrative Employees has a minimal effect on cost savings. It does provide an opportunity to expand customer service to residents by opening earlier and closing later on the days they are open. However, short of workforce reduction, the required transition only has a potential cost savings of very little with possibly saving on sick time Because employees would have a guaranteed day to take off and schedule for appointments. A 40 -hour workweek, whether it is four days or a 40 -hour workweek over five days, is still Paid the same, and so there's no cost savings there. As we are in a shared building, the heating, cooling, electrical costs are still going To be relatively the same with providing access five days a week versus four days a week.
